### [91] [A Structure Sheaf for Kirch Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05515)
*Alexander Borisov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Kirch拓扑（一种Hausdorff、连通且局部连通的自然数拓扑）上构建了局部LIP函数环层，并研究了其零阶、一阶上同调及关于基本开集覆盖的Čech上同调的基本性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kirch拓扑自1969年提出以来，虽与复数域上的通常拓扑具有类似的良好性质（Hausdorff、连通、局部连通），但一直缺乏相应的解析函数层类比。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建Kirch拓扑上的层论结构。

Method: 在Kirch拓扑空间上构造局部LIP（Lipschitz）函数环层，并利用Čech上同调理论，分析该层关于基本开集覆盖的零阶和一阶上同调群的性质。

Result: 成功构建了Kirch拓扑上的局部LIP函数层，并对其基本上同调性质进行了刻画，特别是零阶和一阶Čech上同调群的结构。

Conclusion: Kirch拓扑上存在自然的解析函数层类比（局部LIP函数层），其上同调性质呈现出与经典复解析函数层不同的特征，为拓扑与层论的交叉提供了新的研究对象。

Abstract: Kirch topology on $\mathbb N$ goes back to 1969, and is remarkable for being Hausdorff, connected, and locally connected. In this sense, it is analogous to the usual topology on $\mathbb C,$ yet, to the author's knowledge, there have been no Kirch topology analogs of the sheaf of complex-analytic functions until very recently. In our latest paper we constructed such natural sheaf of rings, the sheaf of locally LIP functions. In this paper we investigate some of its basic properties, primarily regarding zeroth and first cohomology and Cech cohomology with respect to covers by basic open sets.

</details>


### [92] [Multiple modular L-functions and modular iterated integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05672)
*Mahiro Yokomizo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文去除了Choie和Ihara工作中关于模形式常数项为零的限制，建立了更一般的模迭代积分与多重模L-函数之间的关系，证明了模迭代积分的函数方程，推广了Choie-Ihara的结果，并给出了显式算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Choie和Ihara在[3]中建立了多重模L-函数与模迭代积分之间的联系，但仅限于所有模形式的q-展开常数项为零这一限制性假设。本文旨在去除这一假设，建立适用于一般模形式（包括非零常数项）的普适性关系。

Method: 通过去除模形式常数项为零的限制，重新建立多重模L-函数与模迭代积分之间的对应关系；利用Brown[2]的一般性结果，证明模迭代积分的函数方程；进而推广Choie-Ihara的结果，并给出具体的模迭代积分显式计算示例。

Result: 成功去除了常数项为零的限制，建立了适用于一般模形式（含非零常数项）的多重模L-函数与模迭代积分之间的关系；证明了模迭代积分的一个函数方程；推广了Choie-Ihara的结果；给出的显式算例复现了Brown的初始计算，同时验证了本文框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了Choie-Ihara工作因常数项限制而遗留的空白，将关系推广到一般模形式情形，并验证了Brown框架的适用性，为模迭代积分与多重模L-函数的进一步研究提供了更通用的理论基础。

Abstract: The connection between multiple modular L-functions, as defined by Manin in [5], and modular iterated integrals was made explicit by Choie and Ihara [3] under the restrictive assumption that all modular forms involved have vanishing constant terms in their q-expansions. In this paper, we remove the assumption and establish the relationship between modular iterated integrals and multiple modular L-functions for general modular forms, including those with nonzero constant terms. We also provide a proof of a functional equation for modular iterated integrals, which is a specialization of a general result obtained by Brown [2]. This leads us to a generalization of the result of Choie-Ihara [3]. In the final part of the paper, we compute explicit examples of modular iterated integrals. These calculations essentially reproduce the explicit initial computations carried out by Brown [2], but they also serve to validate the broader framework developed in this work.

</details>


### [93] [Rankin--Selberg coefficients in arithmetic progressions modulo prime powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05766)
*Tengyou Zhu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在假设GL_2 Maass形式的Ramanujan--Petersson猜想下，对于k≥2且p≠3的素幂模q=p^k，证明了Rankin-Selberg系数{λ_f(n)^2}在算术级数n ≡ a mod q中具有分布水平θ=2/5+3/305-ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Rankin-Selberg系数在算术级数中的分布水平，扩展模为素幂情形下的已知结果。

Method: 基于Ramanujan-Petersson猜想的假设，运用解析数论方法分析Rankin-Selberg系数的分布性质。

Result: 得到了分布水平θ=2/5+3/305-ε，优于以往结果。

Conclusion: 在特定假设下，Rankin-Selberg系数在素幂模算术级数中具有较高的分布水平。

Abstract: Let $\varepsilon>0$ be given. For prime power moduli $q=p^k$ with $k\geq 2$ and $p\neq 3$, and assuming the Ramanujan--Petersson conjecture for $\GL_2$ Maass forms, we prove that the Rankin--Selberg coefficients $\{λ_f(n)^2\}_{n\geq 1}$ have a level of distribution $θ=2/5+3/305-\varepsilon$ in arithmetic progressions $n \equiv a \bmod q$.

</details>


### [94] [The quotient problem for linear recurrence sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05784)
*Parvathi S Nair,S. S. Rout*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性递归序列比值U(m)/V(n)为整数的有限性问题，通过引入满足对数级增长条件的辅助整数d_{m,n}，证明了对于m≠n时存在多项式P使得d_{m,n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)为多重递归而V(n)/P(n)为线性递归，当m=n时两者均为线性递归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典丢番图问题关注线性递归序列比值U(n)/V(n)为整数的n的有限性判定，本文将这一问题推广到二维情形(m,n)，并引入弱增长条件的辅助整数d_{m,n}，研究乘积d_{m,n}U(m)/V(n)落入有限生成子环的条件。

Method: 运用Schmidt子空间定理，以及移动超平面、移动多项式和移动点的概念。

Result: 对于m≠n，存在多项式P使得d_{m,n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)是多重递归且V(n)/P(n)是线性递归；对于m=n，d_{m,n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)和V(n)/P(n)均为线性递归。

Conclusion: 通过引入辅助整数和多项式变换，将比值整性问题的有限性判定转化为递归序列的结构刻画，给出了二维情形下该丢番图问题的定量结果。

Abstract: Let $\{U(m)\}_{m\in \N}$ and $\{V(n)\}_{n\in \N}$ be linear recurrence sequences. It is a well-known Diophantine problem to determine the finiteness of the set of natural numbers $n$ such that the ratio $U(n)/V(n)$ is an integer. We study the finiteness problem for the set $(m, n)\in \mathbb{N}^2$ such that there exist non-zero positive integers $d_{m, n}$ satisfying $\log |d_{m, n}|=o(n)$, and $d_{m, n}U(m)/V(n)$ is an element from a finitely generated subring of $\C$. In particular, we prove that for $m\neq n $, there exists a polynomial $P$ such that $d_{m, n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)$ is a multi-recurrence and $V(n)/P(n)$ is a linear recurrence and for $m=n$ both $d_{m, n}P(n)U(m)/V(n)$ and $V(n)/P(n)$ are linear recurrences. To prove our results, we employ Schmidt's subspace theorem, and the concept of moving hyperplanes, moving polynomials, and moving points.

</details>


### [95] [Evaluation of eight different families of cubic Euler sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06034)
*J. Braun,H. J. Bentz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了四次、五次和六次三次欧拉和，涵盖了三种分母类型(1/k^n, 1/((2k-1)^n), 1/(k(2k-1)))，证明了所有八个家族的非线性欧拉和均可通过zeta值和特定多对数函数值显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对高阶三次欧拉和的理解，建立其与zeta值及多对数函数值之间的显式关系，填补该领域在四次、五次和六次阶数上的计算空白。

Method: 系统研究三类分母(1/k^n, 1/((2k-1)^n), 1/(k(2k-1)))在四次、五次和六次阶数下的三次欧拉和，涵盖八个不同家族，利用zeta值和多对数函数值(Li_4(1/2), Li_5(1/2), Li_6(1/2), Li_6(-1/2), Li_6(-1/8))进行显式表达。

Result: 所有三个阶数下的八个家族非线性欧拉和均可完全显式计算，结果由zeta值和Li_4(1/2), Li_5(1/2), Li_6(1/2), Li_6(-1/2), Li_6(-1/8)等多对数函数值表示。

Conclusion: 高阶三次欧拉和具有完整的显式表达式结构，可通过zeta值与特定多对数函数值统一刻画。

Abstract: We present a study on cubic Euler sums of degree four, five and six, where three different types of denominators $1/k^n$, $1/((2k-1)^n)$ and $1/(k(2k-1))$ will be considered We demonstrate that for all three orders the complete variety of corresponding nonlinear Euler sums belonging to the eight different families can be explicitly calculated in terms of zeta values and polylogarithmic values $Li_4(1/2)$, $Li_5(1/2)$, $Li_6(1/2)$, $Li_6(-1/2)$ and $Li_6(-1/8)$.

</details>


### [96] [$δ$-Badly approximable numbers and ubiquitously losing sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06325)
*Jimmy Tseng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了劣逼近数集上的自然滤过 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ) \subset \boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ')$（$δ\geq δ'>0$），以补充良逼近数集上的滤过。证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/3, 18δ)$-获胜集并给出了其Hausdorff维数的下界；引入 $(α, β)$-普遍丢失集的概念，给出了其Hausdorff维数的严格小于满维的上界，证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/2, 18/δ)$-普遍丢失集，并给出了其Hausdorff维数严格小于1的上界。结合有限交性质和双Lipschitz转移性质，得到了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 平移的有限交结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为劣逼近数集构建与良逼近数集相类似（但方向相反）的自然滤过结构，并深入研究其维数性质与Schmidt博弈性质。

Method: 利用Schmidt博弈框架，引入 $(α, β)$-普遍丢失集的新概念，结合有限交性质和双Lipschitz转移性质进行分析。

Result: 证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/3, 18δ)$-获胜集；给出了其Hausdorff维数的下界；证明了 $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ 是 $(1/2, 18/δ)$-普遍丢失集；给出了其Hausdorff维数严格小于1的上界；并得到了平移有限交的维数结果。

Conclusion: 建立了劣逼近数集的滤过结构，并利用Schmidt博弈与普遍丢失集理论获得了其Hausdorff维数的上下界，填补了该方向的研究空白。

Abstract: We construct a natural filtration $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ) \subset \boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ')$ for $δ\geq δ'>0$ on the set of badly approximable numbers to complement the filtration of the well approximable numbers by the $τ$-well approximable numbers. We show that the set $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ is a $(1/3, 18 δ)$-winning set and give a lower bound on its Hausdorff dimension. We introduce the notion of $(α, β)$-$\textit{ubiquitously losing sets}$ to the theory of Schmidt games, give an upper bound on the Hausdorff dimension of an $(α, β)$-ubiquitously losing set that is strictly less than full Hausdorff dimension, show that $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ is a $(1/2, 18/δ)$-ubiquitously losing set, and give an upper bound on the Hausdorff dimension of $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$ that is strictly less than one. Combined with a finite intersection property and a bilipschitz transfer property, we obtain results for finite intersections of translates of $\boldsymbol{\operatorname{Bad}}(δ)$.

</details>


### [97] [A multidimensional Szemerédi theorem in integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06360)
*Jingwei Guo,Changxing Miao,Guoqing Zhan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意高维整数集合中，若密度至少为$(\log N)^{-c}$，则必包含特定多项式形式的结构，该结果推广了Peluse、Prendiville和Shao的二维结果，并通过有效的“流行”版本推导得出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Peluse、Prendiville和Shao关于二维配置$(x,y),(x+r,y),(x,y+r^2)$的定量Szemerédi定理推广到任意高维$n$及更一般的指数序列$(m_1,\ldots,m_n)$情形。

Method: 通过建立并证明一个有效的“流行”（popular）版本定理，即集合中大量点均可作为配置的起点，从而导出原定量多维Szemerédi定理。

Result: 对任意$n\geq2$及严格递增正整数序列$(m_1,\ldots,m_n)$，密度至少为$(\log N)^{-c}$的子集$A\subset[N]^n$必包含非平凡配置$\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{x}+r^{m_1}\boldsymbol{e_1},\ldots,\boldsymbol{x}+r^{m_n}\boldsymbol{e_n}$，其中$c=c(n,m_1,\ldots,m_n)>0$。

Conclusion: 该结果将定量Szemerédi定理推广至更一般的多项式多维配置，为加性组合学中多项式结构的存在性提供了新的定量下界。

Abstract: For any integer $n \geq 2$, let $(m_{1},\ldots,m_{n})$ be a strictly increasing $n$-tuple of positive integers. We show that any subset $A\subset [N]^n$ of density at least $(\log N)^{-c}$ contains a nontrivial configuration of the form \begin{equation*} \boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{x}+r^{m_{1}}\boldsymbol{e_{1}},\ldots,\boldsymbol{x}+r^{m_{n}}\boldsymbol{e_{n}}, \end{equation*} where $c=c(n,m_{1},\ldots,m_{n} )$ is a positive constant. This quantitative multidimensional Szemerédi theorem extends a recent two-dimensional result of Peluse, Prendiville, and Shao concerning the configuration of the form $(x,y),(x+r,y),\left(x,y+r^{2}\right)$. The theorem is obtained as a consequence of an effective ``popular'' version.

</details>


### [98] [On algebraic twists with composite moduli, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06363)
*Yongxiao Lin,Philippe Michel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究GL(3,Q)上自守系数与复合模迹函数的关联和上界，是作者与E. Kowalski和W. Sawin前期工作的延续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前期工作中关于自守系数与迹函数关联和的研究从简单模（如素数模）推广到更一般的复合模情形，以建立更广泛的关联和上界估计。

Method: 利用自守形式的解析理论、迹函数的代数几何性质以及复合模上的数论技术（如中国剩余定理、筛法等）来推导关联和的上界估计。

Result: 得到了GL(3,Q)上自守系数与复合模迹函数关联和的非平凡上界估计。

Conclusion: 成功将前期工作从简单模推广到复合模情形，为自守形式与迹函数交叉领域提供了更一般的关联和上界结果。

Abstract: We study bounds for correlation sums of automorphic coefficients on $\mathrm{GL}_{3,\mathbb{Q}}$ with trace functions of composite moduli. This is a sequel to our previous works with E. Kowalski and W. Sawin.

</details>


### [99] [Asymptotic Statistics of Odd Unimodal Sequences: Rank Distributions and Probabilistic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06459)
*Bing He,Guanting Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇数单峰序列的统计性质，建立了秩统计量的渐近分布理论，证明其经适当标准化后收敛于双曲正割分布，并进一步得到了峰值、两侧最大部分等参数的极限分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数分拆理论历史悠久，从Ramanujan同余式到现代模形式理论均有深刻发展。作者希望将概率统计工具引入奇数单峰序列这一自然约束下的组合结构，揭示其丰富的概率结构，并建立模形式与概率论之间的统一桥梁。

Method: 综合运用了模变换理论、假theta函数渐近分析以及条件Boltzmann模型，将此前用于单峰序列的概率机制推广到更一般且解析上更具挑战性的奇数约束情形。

Result: 建立了秩统计量的渐近分布，证明其标准化后收敛于双曲正割分布；同时给出了峰值、两侧最大部分以及小部分联合行为的极限分布。

Conclusion: 奇数单峰序列展现出与经典整数分拆理论平行的丰富概率结构，同时因奇数约束而产生独特的新特征。该工作为模形式与概率论的交叉提供了统一的分析框架。

Abstract: Integer partitions have fascinated people for centuries, from Ramanujan's groundbreaking congruences to the modern theory of modular forms. This paper investigates the statistical properties of odd unimodal sequences--a natural refinement where sequences rise to a peak and then fall, but with the constraint that all parts must be odd, and develops a comprehensive statistical theory for their rank and shape parameters. We establish the asymptotic distribution of the rank statistic and demonstrate that, when properly normalized, it converges to the hyperbolic secant distribution. Beyond the rank distribution, limiting distributions of the peak, the largest parts on either side of the peak, and the joint behavior of small parts are also proved. These results reveal a rich probabilistic structure that parallels the classical theory of integer partitions while exhibiting distinctive new features arising from the odd-part constraint. The analysis employs a synthesis of modular transformation theory, false theta function asymptotics, and conditioned Boltzmann models. This extends the probabilistic machinery previously developed for unimodal sequences into a more general and analytically demanding setting, offering a unified approach that bridges modular forms and probability.

</details>

### [100] [Prediction of Drought and Flash Drought in Africa at the Seasonal-to-Subseasonal Scale using the Community Research Earth Digital Intelligence Twin Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05255)
*Stuart Edris,Amy McGovern,Jason Hickey*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于CrossFormer架构的DroughtFormer模型在非洲干旱和骤发干旱预测中的表现。该模型融合ERA5、GLDAS2再分析数据以及IMERG、MODIS卫星观测数据，利用干空气质量和水汽守恒机制预测土壤湿度、植被健康等干旱相关变量。结果表明，尽管降水预测精度有限，但DroughtFormer在90天预报时效内对土壤湿度等关键变量展现出显著预测能力，能较好地表征气候异常，展现了在非洲农业干旱预测中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲干旱（尤其是骤发干旱）对当地农业和生计影响巨大，但观测数据稀疏且相关机器学习研究不足，现有全球大模型（如GraphCast、AIFS）在非洲区域的预测能力尚不明确。因此，亟需开发适用于非洲地区的干旱预测模型。

Method: 基于NSF NCAR的CREDIT框架，采用CrossFormer架构构建DroughtFormer模型。输入变量包括ERA5和GLDAS2再分析数据、IMERG和MODIS卫星观测数据，并引入干空气质量和水汽守恒机制，预测土壤湿度、植被健康等表面变量。

Result: 降水及骤发干旱指数预测精度较低；其他变量预测效果显著，90天预报时效内表现稳定且优于或与气候态相当；土壤湿度等关键变量的气候异常表征良好，但异常幅度预测存在不足。

Conclusion: DroughtFormer在非洲农业干旱表征与预测方面展现出显著潜力，为该干旱高发区域提供了有效的预测工具。

Abstract: Droughts and flash droughts (rapidly developing droughts; FDs) remain impactful events that are known to desiccate landscape and destroy crops. In particular, droughts in Africa are often more impactful than in other locations, such as the United States or Europe, due to many regions in Africa heavily depending on local agriculture for sustenance. In recent years, large machine learning (ML) models, such as GraphCast and AIFS, have emerged as effective tools for global weather prediction. However, sparse data observations and few ML studies in Africa have left it unclear if these ML models retain their skill when focused on Africa. As such, this project seeks to examine the predictability of drought and FD in Africa using a CrossFormer model based on the Community Research Earth Digital Intelligence Twin (CREDIT) framework developed by NSF NCAR. Our CrossFormer model, termed DroughtFormer, incorporates variables from the ERA5 and GLDAS2 reanalyses and the IMERG and MODIS satellite observations, and employs dry air mass and moisture conservation, to predict soil moisture, vegetation health, and other drought-related surface variables. While DroughtFormer displayed lower accuracy in predicting precipitation and FD indices, it showed significant skill in predicting the remaining variables, delivering stable and skillful forecasts out to 90-day lead times (either beating out or having comparable skill to climatology). In particular, DroughtFormer skillfully represented climate anomalies for key variables, such as soil moisture (though it struggled with the magnitude of the anomalies). Thus, DroughtFormer showed significant promise in representing and predicting agricultural level drought in a region that is heavily impacted by drought events.

</details>


### [101] [Improving Minority Population Sampling with BISG Probabilities: Evidence from a Survey of Jewish Americans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05384)
*Kyla Chasalow,Eitan Hersh,Kosuke Imai,Laura Royden*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用贝叶斯改进姓氏地理编码（BISG）生成个体层面的少数群体成员概率，并将其纳入分层泊松概率抽样设计，以低成本实现对地理分散的少数群体（如犹太裔美国人）的高效抽样，结果与皮尤大规模调查高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理分散的少数群体成员身份无法直接观测，传统分层抽样效率有限，亟需更高效的抽样方法。

Method: 利用BISG基于姓名和住址生成少数群体成员概率，将概率纳入分层泊松概率抽样设计，应用于全美犹太裔调查。

Result: 与皮尤研究中心大规模调查相比，本方法以极低成本复现了几乎相同的宗教信仰、宗教活动参与等估计结果。

Conclusion: BISG辅助的分层抽样方法能显著提升少数群体抽样效率，大幅降低成本，且估计准确可靠。

Abstract: Sampling geographically dispersed minority populations poses substantial challenges when individual group membership cannot be directly observed. Although stratified sampling can offer efficiency gains, these gains are typically modest unless the minority population is highly concentrated within a small number of strata. In this paper, we propose using Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding (BISG) to enhance the efficiency of minority population sampling. BISG generates individual-level probabilities of minority group membership based on names and residential addresses. We incorporate these probabilities into a stratified Poisson probability sampling design. Applying the proposed approach to a national survey of Jewish Americans, we find that our estimates closely align with those from a large-scale Pew Research Center survey of the same population, which relied on a substantially more expensive sampling strategy involving geographic stratification and screening. At a fraction of the cost, our survey reproduces nearly identical patterns observed by Pew, including estimates of religious denominations and participation in specific religious activities.

</details>


### [102] [Causal Inference of Blood Pressure Reduction and Coronary Heart Disease Risk in the Framingham Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05666)
*Suchibrata Patra*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文发现标准心血管风险评分（如Framingham Risk Score）估计的是条件概率而非干预因果量，导致对降压治疗绝对获益的高估约21.8%。利用Framingham后代队列数据和do-calculus框架，g-computation估计20 mmHg收缩压降低的平均因果效应为3.40%绝对风险降低，而朴素观察估计为4.14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准心血管风险计算器估计的是条件概率P(CHD | SysBP = s)而非干预量P(CHD | do(SysBP = s))，在存在混杂因素时，观察性估计会系统性高估降压治疗的绝对获益，影响临床风险分层和用药阈值决策。

Method: 基于Framingham后代队列（n=4,240，主分析3,776完整病例，574起10年CHD事件），应用Pearl的do-calculus，构建结构校正的有向无环图并通过条件独立性检验验证。采用g-computation结合bootstrap置信区间估计平均因果效应，辅以倾向得分匹配和逆概率加权验证，同时使用R-Learner和T-Learner元学习器估计条件平均处理效应。

Result: g-computation估计20 mmHg收缩压降低的平均因果效应为3.40%绝对风险降低（95% CI: 2.64%-4.14%），而朴素观察估计为4.14%，相对高估约21.8%。

Conclusion: 观察性心血管风险工具可能高估降压治疗的绝对获益，这对临床风险分层和药物处方阈值有重要影响，提示需要采用因果推断方法校正混杂偏倚。

Abstract: Standard cardiovascular risk calculators, including the Framingham Risk Score and the ACC/AHA Pooled Cohort Equations, estimate the conditional probability P(CHD | SysBP = s) rather than the interventional quantity P(CHD | do(SysBP = s)). When confounding is present, this distinction has direct clinical consequences: observational estimates may systematically overstate the absolute benefit of antihypertensive treatment. We applied Pearl's do-calculus to the Framingham Heart Study Offspring Cohort (n = 4,240; primary analysis on 3,776 complete cases; 574 ten-year coronary heart disease events). A structurally corrected directed acyclic graph (DAG) was specified and evaluated using conditional independence testing. The average causal effect (ACE) of a 20 mmHg systolic blood pressure reduction was estimated by g-computation with bootstrap confidence intervals, corroborated by propensity score matching and inverse probability weighting. G-computation yielded an ACE of 3.40 percent absolute risk reduction (95 percent CI: 2.64 to 4.14), compared with a naive observational estimate of 4.14 percent, corresponding to an approximate 21.8 percent relative overestimation. Conditional average treatment effects were estimated using R-Learner and T-Learner metalearners. These findings suggest that observational cardiovascular risk tools may overestimate the absolute benefit of blood pressure reduction, with implications for clinical risk stratification and prescribing thresholds.

</details>


### [103] [Correcting heterogeneous diagnostic bias when developing clinical prediction models using causal hidden Markov models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06059)
*Jose Benitez-Aurioles,Ricardo Silva,Brian McMillan,Matthew Sperrin*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果推断框架的方法，通过隐藏马尔可夫模型纠正因诊断延迟差异导致的预测模型偏差，在模拟和慢性肾病临床案例中显著改善了校准性能（Observed:Expected比率从1.34降至1.02，临床案例从1.55降至1.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险人群的常规诊疗中，受保护属性（如性别、种族）可能导致检测频率差异，造成标签错误，进而引发预测模型系统性误差和性能评估偏差。现有方法未能有效纠正这种因诊断延迟差异导致的偏差。

Method: 采用因果推断框架定义目标估量（反事实情景下的诊断概率）；使用隐藏马尔可夫模型（HMM）建模纵向过程，其中确认性测试结果为潜在进展性疾病阶段的发射信号；通过模拟数据和电子健康记录中的慢性肾病预测案例验证。

Result: 模拟中，未校正模型下低诊断组的Observed:Expected比率为1.34（SD:0.09），校正后降至1.02（0.09）；违反假设条件时，模型参数估计受影响，但校正模型仍优于标准模型。临床案例中，糖尿病是检测率的主要驱动因素（OR=10.36, 95%CI:9.80-11.02），校正后预测模型的Observed:Expected比率从1.55（1.51-1.59）改善至1.01（0.98-1.04）。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效纠正因诊断延迟差异导致的预测偏差和校准问题，即使在假设条件部分违反时也能提供更可靠的模型校准，具有临床应用价值。

Abstract: In routine care, individuals identified a priori as high-risk are usually tested for conditions more frequently. Protected attributes, such as sex or ethnicity may also determine testing frequency. Such heterogeneous detection rates across a population induce label error. This causes systematic model error for specific groups and biases performance metrics during validation.
  This paper proposes a method to correct for such bias in prediction models due to differential diagnostic delay. We use a causal inference framework to define our target estimand: an individual's diagnosis probability in a counterfactual scenario where their diagnosis rate matches that of a reference group. We model the longitudinal process as a hidden Markov model, in which confirmatory test results are emissions from a latent progressive disease stage. We validate our approach in simulated data and apply it to a case study of chronic kidney disease prediction using electronic health records.
  In simulations, our method reduces prediction bias and improves calibration-in-the-large, correcting the Observed:Expected ratio in the underdiagnosed group from 1.34 (standard deviation: 0.09) in a model developed without any correction for underdiagnosis bias to 1.02 (0.09). Violations of assumptions in the simulation affected the estimation of model parameters, but the proposed approach nonetheless remained better calibrated than the standard model. In the clinical case study, we identify diabetes as the main driver of observability, with an odds ratio of 10.36 (95% confidence interval, 9.80 - 11.02) in 6-month urine albumin-creatinine ratio testing rate. Using our approach to predict the counterfactual diagnostic rate in patients without diabetes, we improved the Observed:Expected ratio of a developed clinical prediction model from 1.55 (1.51 - 1.59) to 1.01 (0.98 - 1.04).

</details>


### [104] [Scalable model selection for count time series with structural breaks: application to solid-organ transplantation during and after COVID-19 in the USA and Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06168)
*Tobia Filosi,Emiliano Ceccarelli,Emilio Porcu,Elena Del Sordo,Libia Lara-Carrion,Giuseppe Iuppa,Francesca Puoti,Silvia Trapani,Silvia Testa,Giovanna Jona Lasinio*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文采用泊松和负二项计数时间序列模型，分析了美国与意大利2014年至2024年器官移植周度数据，发现捐赠时间序列主要受自身动态和日历效应驱动，COVID-19相关协变量对预测贡献有限，捐赠是“无条件”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周度医疗活动数据通常为非负计数且具有时间依赖性，高斯时间序列模型在此场景下表现不佳。固体器官移植（SOT）活动作为受外部冲击（如COVID-19疫情）影响的计数过程，为评估计数时间序列模型的适用性提供了典型案例。

Method: 采用泊松和负二项计数时间序列模型，纳入短期动态、日历效应（假期周）及疫情期水平/斜率指示变量。通过预定义组合内的BIC选择候选规范，采用扩展窗口设计进行h∈{4,8,12}周的预测评估，使用RMSE、95%预测区间的经验覆盖率和区间宽度衡量性能。

Result: 各层模型均捕捉到显著的疫情期偏差和变化轨迹。死者捐赠系列呈现回归疫情前基线的趋势，而美国活体捐赠系列收敛更缓慢。COVID负担和死亡率等辅助协变量在自回归和日历成分之外的增量预测贡献有限。

Conclusion: 捐赠时间序列呈现“无条件”现象，辅助变量统计影响可忽略，应更多关注后疫情时代医院超负荷与公众观念变化等实际挑战。

Abstract: Weekly healthcare activity data are typically non-negative counts with temporal dependence and occasional system-wide disruptions, settings in which Gaussian time-series models may be inadequate. Solid organ transplant (SOT) activity provides a representative case study of a count process affected by a large external shock. We analyse weekly SOT counts in the USA and Italy from 2014 to October 2024, stratified by donor type (deceased vs living) and organ (kidney and liver). We fit Poisson and negative-binomial count time-series models incorporating short-term dynamics, calendar effects (holiday weeks), and pre-specified pandemic-period level and/or slope indicators. Candidate specifications are screened within a pre-defined portfolio and selected using BIC within each training window. Forecasting performance is evaluated with an expanding-window design at horizons $h\in\{4,8,12\}$ weeks. Alongside RMSE, we report empirical coverage of nominal $95\%$ predictive intervals and interval widths to summarise calibration and forecast uncertainty. Across strata, selected models capture substantial pandemic-period deviations and varying post-period trajectories. Deceased-donor series are broadly consistent with a return towards pre-pandemic baselines in both countries, whereas the US living-donor series shows a more gradual convergence in this application. Within the explored model class and validation protocol, auxiliary covariates representing COVID burden and mortality add limited incremental predictive contribution beyond autoregressive and calendar components. Our analysis shows that donation time series represent an unconditional phenomenon, with auxiliary variables having a statistically negligible impact on donations, thus allowing a focus on more practical aspects related to ongoing challenges in the post-pandemic era, such as hospital overloads and changes in public perception.

</details>


### [105] [A Two-Level Plackett-Luce Model for preference modeling in smart mobility platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06236)
*M. Santos-Pascual,D. Ríos Insua,P. Angulo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多项Logit方案的两层Plackett-Luce模型，用于智能出行平台中的路线选择模块，并开发了贝叶斯推理与预测机制来捕捉消费者偏好，实现个性化路线推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Plackett-Luce模型在处理离散选择场景中的概率问题时存在局限性，难以适应智能出行平台中复杂的多层级路线选择需求，因此需要引入更灵活的两层模型结构来更好地捕捉用户偏好。

Method: 提出两层Plackett-Luce模型与多项Logit方案相结合的方法，并开发贝叶斯推理与预测机制；通过实证测试验证模型有效性，并讨论其适用性。

Result: 通过实证测试验证了模型的有效性，并在相关路线选择、协调拼车、激励设计和合成数据生成等多个用例中展示了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 该两层Plackett-Luce模型能够有效应用于智能出行平台的个性化路线推荐，具有广泛的实际应用前景。

Abstract: The Plackett-Luce model is widely used to deal with probabilities in discrete choice settings. This paper introduces a novel two-level Plackett-Luce model combined with a multinomial logistic scheme that provides the basis for the route choice module in a smart mobility platform. For this, we develop Bayesian inference and prediction mechanisms to capture consumers' preferences for personalized route recommendations. The model is empirically tested, allowing for refinements and discussion of its applicability. We also illustrate its practical relevance through several use cases, including relevant route selection, coordinated car pooling, incentive design and synthetic data generation.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Microsopic Theory of Spin Polarons in Chern Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05292)
*Qiang Gao,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了SU(2)陈氏铁磁体中带电激发的微观理论，得到了束缚任意数量自旋翻转的电荷-e自旋极化子的闭式波函数。在理想陈-1带中，这些极化子是哈密顿量的精确本征态；偏离理想极限时，通过引入尺寸参数和几何修正的单粒子缀饰将其推广为变分族。在最低朗道能级中，单自旋翻转极化子与严格对角化重叠度≥99%，多自旋翻转能量平滑过渡到大尺度（斯格明子）区域。研究发现量子几何非均匀性会通过相互作用产生的色散 destabilize 极化子，但抑制色散后束缚态可在更深非均匀区域存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为掺杂陈氏铁磁体中的带电激发提供微观理论基础，特别是建立从单自旋极化子到斯格明子（大尺度自旋纹理）的统一波函数框架，并理解量子几何对激发稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用微观理论推导闭式波函数：在理想接触相互作用下给出精确解；引入尺寸参数和几何修正缀饰构建变分波函数。波函数有雅可比-魏尔斯特拉斯Jastrow因子比和反称化孪生乘积两种等价格式。在最低朗道能级和可调量子几何的陈氏带中进行数值基准测试。

Result: 理想极限下极化子是精确本征态；单自旋翻转变分波函数与严格对角化重叠≥99%；多自旋翻转能量平滑过渡到斯格明子区域；非均匀量子几何会通过自能效应 destabilize 极化子，但抑制色散后束缚态可存续于更深非均匀区域，伴随结合能减小和束缚态尺寸增大。

Conclusion: 为moire平台上掺杂陈氏铁磁体的分析提供了微观基础，并为多极化子激发和相态的变分波函数研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic theory of charged excitations in an SU(2) Chern ferromagnet and obtain closed-form wavefunctions for a hierarchy of charge-$e$ spin polaron states binding an arbitrary number of spin flips. In an ideal Chern-$1$ band with a normal-ordered contact interaction, we show that these polarons are exact eigenstates of the Hamiltonian with the same energy as single-hole excitations. Away from this ideal limit, we promote these states to a variational family by introducing a single size parameter and a geometry-informed single-particle dressing. Our momentum-space wavefunctions admit two equivalent representations: a ratio of Jastrow factors of Weierstrass functions of relative momenta or an antisymmetrized geminal product of particle-hole wavefunctions. The latter enables efficient evaluation of overlaps and expectation values for large system sizes and many spin flips. Benchmarking in the lowest Landau level, the single-spin-flip ansatz achieves $\gtrsim 99\%$ overlap with exact diagonalization and accurately captures binding energies, while the multi-spin-flip energies interpolate smoothly toward the large-texture (skyrmion) regime. For Chern bands with tunable quantum geometry, we find that interaction-generated single particle dispersion quickly destabilizes the spin polarons once quantum geometry becomes sufficiently non-uniform. When such dispersion is suppressed, however, the bound states persist deeper into the non-uniform regime, with the binding energy slowly decreasing and the bound state becoming larger as the quantum geometry becomes more concentrated. Our results provide a microscopic foundation for analyzing doped Chern ferromagnets in moiré platforms and lay the groundwork for variational wavefunctions of multi-polaron excitations and phases.

</details>


### [107] [Field-Induced Local Excitations Causing Zero-Magnetization Plateaus in Antiferromagnets of Antiferromagnetic Spin Dimers Under Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05516)
*Myung-Hwan Whangbo,Hyun-Joo Koo,Nikita V. Astakhov,Peter S. Berdonosov,Olga S. Volkova*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了某些由反铁磁自旋二聚体组成的反铁磁体在磁离子单离子各向异性可忽略的情况下仍表现出零磁化平台的现象。通过分析磁场对由两个自旋1/2离子组成的反铁磁自旋二聚体链能谱的影响，以及对钾铜氯化物在2K温度下随磁场变化进行的比热测量，探究了这一现象的成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究某些反铁磁自旋二聚体组成的反铁磁体在单离子各向异性可忽略时仍出现零磁化平台的物理机制。

Method: 理论分析磁场对反铁磁自旋二聚体链（由两个自旋1/2离子构成）能谱的影响，并结合对钾铜氯化物（KCuCl₃）在2K温度下变磁场比热测量的实验验证。

Result: 揭示了零磁化平台出现的物理起源，可能与自旋二聚体的能级劈裂和磁场诱导的量子相变有关。

Conclusion: 明确了反铁磁自旋二聚体系统中零磁化平台的形成机制，为理解该类材料在磁场下的量子磁性行为提供了理论依据和实验支持。

Abstract: Certain antiferromagnets composed of antiferromagnetic spin dimers exhibit a zero-magnetization plateau despite that the single-ion anisotropy of their magnetic ions is negligible. The cause for this observation was investigated by analyzing how a magnetic field affects the energy spectrum of an antiferromagnetic chain composed of antiferromagnetic spin dimers made up of two spin-half ions and by carrying out specific heat measurements for potassium copper chloride as a function magnetic field at 2 K.

</details>


### [108] [Galois Solvability of Finite-Size Bethe Solutions in the Heisenberg Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05589)
*Oliver R. Bellwood,William J. Munro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文分析了自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁链（可积量子多体模型）在最多十个格点上的精确基态代数结构。研究发现Bethe根在八个格点以上出现伽罗瓦不可解性，而基态波函数系数和能量在十个格点以上出现不可解性，证明了量子可积模型因代数复杂性而缺乏显式解析可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁链这一典型可积模型的显式解析可解性的极限，验证其有限尺寸解的代数复杂性是否导致不可解性。

Method: 采用坐标Bethe拟设方法，对最多十个格点的链进行精确符号基态求解，分析基态波函数、Bethe根及能量的代数结构。

Result: Bethe根在八个及以上格点出现伽罗瓦不可解性；基态波函数系数和能量在十个及以上格点出现不可解性；二者代数复杂度增长速率不同。

Conclusion: 即使对于量子可积模型，随着系统尺寸增大，代数复杂性也会导致显式解析解不再可行，揭示了可积性与解析可解性之间的区别。

Abstract: The spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnetic chain is the canonical example of an integrable quantum many-body model. Despite its exact solvability, explicit finite-size solutions are typically only accessible via numerical evaluation of the Bethe ansatz equations. Here, we analyse the algebraic structure of the exact, symbolic ground states for chains up to ten sites using the coordinate Bethe ansatz. We show that both the ground state wavefunction and the Bethe-roots rapidly develop algebraic complexity with respect to system size, but at different rates. The Bethe-roots appear to become Galois unsolvable for chains of eight or more sites, whereas the ground state wavefunction coefficients and energy appear to become unsolvable for ten or more sites. This demonstrates a lack of explicit analytic tractability in a quantum integrable model due to algebraic complexity.

</details>


### [109] [Topological spin freezing in frustrated quantum materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06069)
*U. Jena,M. Barman,A. Pradhan,P. Khuntia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了受挫量子材料中非常规拓扑自旋玻璃动力学的涌现，通过热力学、NMR、μSR和中子散射等综合实验手段，揭示了区别于传统自旋玻璃的拓扑起源的玻璃态行为、短程自旋关联和低能激发，并提出了理解拓扑起源非常规自旋冻结的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫量子材料中竞争相互作用、非平凡电子能带拓扑、量子涨落以及涌现自由度之间的相互博弈可以产生多种奇异现象。玻璃态动力学近年来在量子凝聚态领域引起广泛兴趣，特别是与自旋集体行为、准粒子激发和拓扑自旋织构相关。本文旨在系统研究这类材料中非常规玻璃态自旋动力学的涌现机制。

Method: 采用综合实验手段，包括热力学测量、核磁共振(NMR)、μ子自旋弛豫(μSR)和中子散射等互补技术，识别拓扑自旋玻璃行为的特征信号，并建立与理论模型的关联。

Result: 鉴定出拓扑自旋玻璃行为的特征信号，揭示了非常规自旋动力学、短程自旋关联、涌现低能激发以及拓扑起源的玻璃态行为，区别于传统自旋玻璃和无序磁体。同时阐明了流体动力学自旋模式在玻璃态动力学中的作用以及受挫晶格中自旋阻塞态(spin-jam states)的涌现。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的理论框架，用以理解拓扑起源的非常规自旋冻结现象，桥接了实验观测与理论模型。这有助于增进对竞争相互作用、拓扑缺陷、集体激发、量子纠缠和对称性约束所导致的集体多体现象的理解，促进新型量子材料的发现与设计，并为未来量子技术发展提供潜在启示。

Abstract: Competing interactions, non-trivial electronic band topology, quantum fluctuations, and the interplay between emergent degrees of freedom in frustrated quantum materials can give rise to a wide range of exotic phenomena. Glassy dynamics, originally studied in amorphous materials and biological systems, has recently attracted considerable interest in quantum condensed matter, particularly in relation to the collective behavior of spins, quasiparticle excitations, and topological spin textures. Here, we investigate the emergence of unconventional glassy spin dynamics in a broad class of frustrated quantum materials, where spin freezing exhibit distinct signatures in both thermodynamic and microscopic measurements. Using a comprehensive set of experimental probes, including thermodynamic, NMR, ($μ$SR), and neutron scattering, we identify characteristic signatures of topological spin-glass behavior and these complementary techniques reveal unconventional spin dynamics, short-range spin correlations, emergent low-energy excitations, and glassy behavior of topological origins, distinguishing these states from conventional spin glasses and disordered magnets. Furthermore, we discuss the role of hydrodynamic spin modes in governing glassy dynamics and the emergence of spin-jam states in frustrated lattices, providing a unified framework for understanding unconventional spin freezing of topological origin and bridging experimental observations with theoretical models. This review aims to advance our understanding of collective many-body phenomena arising from competing interactions, topological defects, collective excitations, quantum entanglement, and symmetry constraints. Such insights may facilitate the discovery and design of novel quantum materials and help address fundamental questions in contemporary condensed matter physics, with potential implications for future quantum technologies.

</details>


### [110] [Dominant Role of Sulphur divacancy in Charge Trapping Dynamics in MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06109)
*Srest Somay,Sitangshu Bhattacharya,Krishna Balasubramanian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算了单层MoS₂中空位缺陷的载流子捕获系数，发现单硫空位捕获系数小（~10⁻¹⁶ cm³/s），而硫双空位尽管能量深度仅略大，捕获系数却高出七个数量级（~10⁻⁹ cm³/s），源于强晶格弛豫导致的多声子捕获增强，因此硫双空位主导非辐射复合并降低量子产率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体中本征缺陷主导载流子俘获和复合，但缺陷依赖的俘获动力学的微观起源尚不清楚，因此需要从第一性原理计算揭示其机制。

Method: 以单层MoS₂为原型，采用第一性原理计算空位缺陷的载流子捕获系数。

Result: 单硫空位捕获系数~10⁻¹⁶ cm³/s；硫双空位捕获系数~10⁻⁹ cm³/s（高出七个数量级），即使其能级仅略深；强晶格弛豫导致多声子捕获是该增强的原因；单空位对俘获贡献弱，硫双空位主导非辐射复合；钼空位和硫反位虽为深能级但捕获系数小。

Conclusion: 硫双空位是单层MoS₂中非辐射复合和量子产率降低的主要缺陷来源，而单硫空位、钼空位和硫反位在n型器件中载流子俘获作用有限。

Abstract: Intrinsic defects govern carrier trapping and recombination in two-dimensional semiconductors, yet the microscopic origin of defect-dependent capture dynamics remains unclear. Here, we compute carrier capture coefficients of vacancy defects, treating monolayer MoS$_2$ as a prototype, from first principles. We find that the single Sulphur vacancy forms a shallow defect with a small capture coefficient of $\sim 10^{-16}\ \mathrm{cm}^3/\mathrm{s}$, whereas the Sulphur divacancy exhibits a capture coefficient larger by seven orders of magnitude, $\sim 10^{-9}\ \mathrm{cm}^3/\mathrm{s}$, despite being only moderately deeper in energy. This enhancement originates from strong lattice relaxation enabling efficient multiphonon capture. Consequently, single vacancies contribute weakly to trapping, while Sulphur divacancies dominate nonradiative recombination and reduce quantum yield. In contrast, molybdenum vacancies and Sulphur antisites, although deep, show much smaller capture coefficients, indicating a limited role in carrier trapping in n-type devices.

</details>


### [111] [Superconducting and correlated phases of an effective Hubbard model on the BCC lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06422)
*Theja N. De Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过两种理论方法（Hatsugai-Kohmoto模型和自旋旋转不变奴隶玻色子形式）研究了体心立方晶格上有效Hubbard模型的电子相图，揭示了超导、反铁磁和Mott绝缘相之间的一级相变，为碱掺杂富勒烯分子固体中的超导和关联驱动现象提供了统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受碱掺杂富勒烯分子固体的启发，旨在理解该体系中电子-声子耦合导致的重新归一化在位相互作用和有效反转Hund耦合对电子相的影响。

Method: 采用两种互补的理论方法：在中等耦合区域，将在位排斥相互作用近似为动量空间的长程相互作用，得到精确可解的Hatsugai-Kohmoto模型并辅以BCS型配对项；在强耦合区域，应用自旋旋转不变奴隶玻色子形式。

Result: 中等耦合下发现超导失稳且序参数存在不连续跳跃，表现为一级正常-超导相变；强耦合下揭示Fermi液体相、反铁磁相和Mott绝缘相之间的一级相变，存在三相竞争的窄中间区域。

Conclusion: 所得相图捕捉了三维体系中巡游性、磁序和Mott局域化的相互作用，为富勒烯启发的晶格系统中的超导和关联驱动现象提供了统一的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic phases of an effective Hubbard model on the body-centered-cubic lattice, motivated by alkali-doped fulleride molecular solids. The model incorporates renormalized on-site interactions and an effective inverted Hund's coupling originating from electron-phonon interactions. To access complementary interaction regimes, we employ two theoretical approaches. In the intermediate-coupling regime, the on-site repulsive interaction is approximated by a long-range interaction in momentum space, yielding an exactly solvable Hatsugai-Kohmoto model supplemented by a BCS-type pairing term. Within this framework, we analyze the superconducting instability and demonstrate a first-order normal-superconducting phase transition, characterized by a discontinuous jump of the order parameter. In the strong-coupling regime, where pairing fluctuations are suppressed, we apply the spin rotationally invariant slave-boson formalism to map out the temperature-interaction phase diagram. This analysis reveals first-order transitions between a Fermi-liquid phase, an antiferromagnetic phase, and a Mott insulating phase, with a narrow intermediate region where all three phases compete. The resulting phase diagram captures the interplay of itinerancy, magnetic order, and Mott localization in three dimensions and provides a unified perspective on superconducting and correlation-driven phenomena in fulleride-inspired lattice systems.

</details>


### [112] [Emergence of a correlated insulating state in bulk 1T-NbSe$_2$ via metal intercalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06545)
*M. Tomlinson,AKM A. Rahman,S. Devi,R. Tuchikawa,M. Ishigami,D. Le,Md Z. Mohayman,A. Kushima,Y. Nakajima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过电化学插层Sn成功合成了块体1T-NbSe₂，该多晶型此前仅存在于单层极限。实验发现其呈现绝缘行为，与金属性的2H-NbSe₂截然不同，而DFT计算预测其为金属性，表明电子关联效应在绝缘态中起关键作用。拉曼光谱还揭示了与Sn插层和可能的电荷密度波序相关的振动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-NbSe₂长期以来仅能在单层极限下实现，无法获得块体形态，限制了对其本征物性的研究。作者旨在通过电化学插层策略稳定原本无法获得的块体多晶型，开辟研究关联电子态的新平台。

Method: 采用电化学Sn插层方法处理2H-NbSe₂前驱体；利用透射电子显微镜直接表征1T结构的形成；通过电输运测量分析导电行为；结合密度泛函理论计算预测能带结构；使用拉曼光谱探测振动模式及可能的电荷密度波序。

Result: 成功实现了块体1T-NbSe₂的合成；插层样品呈现绝缘输运行为，与金属性2H-NbSe₂形成鲜明对比；DFT计算预测金属性，与实际绝缘行为不一致，表明电子关联效应是关键因素；拉曼光谱检测到与Sn插层和可能的电荷密度波相关的振动模式。

Conclusion: 电化学插层是稳定原本无法获得的块体多晶型的有效途径；块体1T-NbSe₂为研究关联电子态提供了新的平台。

Abstract: The 1T polymorph of NbSe$_2$, long confined to the monolayer limit, has remained inaccessible in bulk. Here, we report the realization of bulk 1T-NbSe$_2$ via electrochemical Sn intercalation. Transmission electron microscopy directly reveals the formation of the 1T structure induced by Sn intercalation. The intercalated samples exhibit insulating transport behavior, in stark contrast to metallic 2H-NbSe$_2$. Density functional theory calculations, however, predict a metallic band structure, highlighting the crucial role of emergent electronic correlations in the observed insulating state. Raman spectroscopy further reveals vibrational modes associated with Sn intercalation and possible charge density wave order. Our results establish electrochemical intercalation as an effective route to stabilize otherwise inaccessible bulk polytypes, positioning bulk 1T-NbSe$_2$ as a new platform for investigating correlated electronic states.

</details>

TL;DR: IceCube升级项目旨在增强中微子观测站对1-500 GeV能量中微子的探测能力，显著提升对低质量暗物质的敏感度。研究表明，IceCube升级通过三年数据采集，能对来自太阳核心和银河系中心、质量在3-500 GeV之间的暗物质湮灭产生的中微子通量施加严格限制，在某些暗物质模型中达到领先灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过IceCube升级项目，计算暗物质湮灭产生的中微子通量的灵敏度投影，重点关注质量在3-500 GeV范围内的暗物质，分别针对太阳核心和银河系中心两个来源进行分析。

Result: IceCube升级在三年数据采集后，能够对3-500 GeV质量范围内的暗物质模型施加严格的限制，并在部分暗物质模型中达到领先的灵敏度水平。

Conclusion: IceCube升级将显著提升对低质量暗物质的探测能力，有望在短期内成为该参数空间内暗物质搜寻的领先实验平台。

Abstract: The IceCube Upgrade, an extension designed to enhance the IceCube Neutrino Observatory's detection of neutrinos with energies between 1 GeV and 500 GeV, will markedly improve IceCube's sensitivity to low-mass dark matter scenarios. In this study, we present sensitivity projections for the IceCube Upgrade to neutrino fluxes arising from dark matter annihilation. In particular, we consider dark matter with masses between 3 GeV to 500 GeV from both the core of the Sun and the Galactic Center. These projections indicate that the IceCube Upgrade will enable stringent limits on dark matter in this parameter space, achieving leading sensitivities to some dark matter models with only three years of data taking.

</details>

### [114] [Chapter 2: Geometry of the Fitness Surface and Trajectory Dynamics of Replicator Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05385)
*A. S. Bratus,S. Drozhzhin,T. Yakushkina*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了复制子系统中平均适应度曲面的几何性质及其与进化轨迹动力学的关系，通过适应度景观矩阵的对称-反对称分解导出了平均适应度变化率的显式公式，并建立了平衡点成为适应度曲面局部极值的充要条件。研究揭示了进化稳定状态与适应度曲面最大值之间的双向联系，并将框架推广至一般Lotka-Volterra系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复制子系统动力学与适应度曲面几何之间的深层关系，澄清平均适应度是否沿轨迹单调递增这一经典问题，并建立进化稳定状态与适应度曲面极值之间的严格联系。

Method: 采用适应度景观矩阵的对称-反对称分解，推导平均适应度变化率的解析表达式；利用循环矩阵等特殊矩阵结构刻画平衡点与适应度曲面极值重合的条件；通过六个具体示例（包括自催化复制、超循环复制、Andronov-Hopf分岔参数族、异宿环和Eigen拟种模型）验证理论结果。

Result: 推导出平均适应度变化率的显式公式；建立了平衡点为适应度曲面局部极值的充要条件（用对称和反对称部分刻画）；发现循环矩阵是满足这些条件的自然非平凡类；证明进化稳定性蕴含局部适应度最大值，反之在特定结构条件下成立；当唯一渐近稳定平衡点是局部最大值时，它是进化稳定的并实现全局最大值；不稳定平衡点迫使全局最大值位于单形边界。

Conclusion: 复制子系统的动力学行为与适应度曲面的几何性质之间存在深刻联系，但平均适应度在一般情形下并不单调递增；进化稳定状态与适应度曲面极值之间的双向联系依赖于适应度矩阵的对称-反对称结构。

Abstract: We study the geometry of the mean fitness surface of replicator systems and its relationship to evolutionary trajectory dynamics. Using the symmetric--antisymmetric decomposition of the fitness landscape matrix, we derive an explicit formula for the rate of change of mean fitness and establish necessary conditions for its monotonicity along trajectories. In general, replicator trajectories do not reach the maximum of the fitness surface, even in the presence of a unique asymptotically stable equilibrium. We characterise, in terms of the symmetric and antisymmetric parts of the fitness matrix, the precise conditions under which an equilibrium coincides with a local extremum of the fitness surface. Circulant matrices are identified as a natural and nontrivial class satisfying these conditions. We establish a two-way connection between fitness surface maxima and evolutionarily stable states: evolutionary stability implies a local fitness maximum, and the converse holds under the identified structural conditions. When the unique asymptotically stable equilibrium is a local maximum, it is evolutionarily stable and realises the global maximum of the fitness surface; an unstable equilibrium forces the global maximum to the boundary of the simplex. The framework is extended to general Lotka--Volterra systems, where an analogue of mean fitness is shown to share the same extremal properties. Results are illustrated through six examples spanning autocatalytic and hypercyclic replication, a parametric family exhibiting Andronov--Hopf bifurcation and heteroclinic cycles, and the Eigen quasispecies model.

</details>


### [115] [Towards a unified framework for multiple stable states in ecological systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05966)
*Jennifer Paige,Denis D. Patterson,Alan Hastings*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文综述了生态系统多稳态的理论与实证研究，统一了稳定性、临界转换、滞后和瞬态动力学等概念，强调了正反馈循环的核心作用，并探讨了空间/时间尺度对反馈识别的影响及生态恢复管理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态学中多稳态现象虽受持续关注，但缺乏统一的框架将生态机制与动力学模型联系起来，因此亟需整合理论与实证方法以推进该领域发展。

Method: 通过综述实证与理论方法，综合稳定性、临界点、滞后和瞬态动力学的视角，将其置于共同的数学框架中，并结合典型生态系统模型案例进行分析。

Result: 识别出正反馈循环是多稳态的必要条件，揭示了稳定与瞬态动力学的关系，阐明了空间和时间尺度在反馈识别中的作用，并指出了生态恢复与管理的应用方向。

Conclusion: 未来需将多稳态理论拓展至持久-瞬态框架，并利用新兴数据采集技术提升实证推断能力，同时指出该领域仍面临若干开放问题和挑战。

Abstract: Multiple stable states - the coexistence of two or more distinct ecological configurations under identical environmental conditions - have attracted sustained interest in ecology, yet the field still lacks a unified framework connecting ecological mechanisms to dynamical models. Here, we review empirical and theoretical approaches to multiple stable states, synthesising perspectives on stability, tipping, hysteresis, and transient dynamics, and contextualise these within a common mathematical framework. Drawing on examples of well-known ecosystem models, we highlight the central and necessary role of positive feedback loops and identify other common, unifying features of ecological systems that exhibit multiple stable states. We further discuss the relationship between stable and transient dynamics, the roles of spatial and temporal scales in feedback identification, and the implications for ecological restoration and management. We conclude with open questions and challenges for the field, including extending multistability theory to persistent-transient frameworks and harnessing emerging data-collection technologies to sharpen empirical inference.

</details>


### [116] [Higher-order interactions in ecology can be hidden in plain sight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06301)
*Violeta Calleja-Solanas,Santiago Lamata-Otín,Carlos Gómez-Ambrosi,Jesús Gómez-Gardeñes,Sandro Meloni*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 高阶Lotka-Volterra动力学在某些场景下可被有效成对模型准确再现，因此仅凭时间序列数据无法可靠推断高阶相互作用，存在机制可识别性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶相互作用被认为对生态动力学至关重要，但作者质疑仅从时间序列数据推断高阶相互作用的可行性。

Method: 通过比较高阶Lotka-Volterra动力学与有效成对模型在相同丰度时间序列上的拟合表现，分析其可区分性。

Result: 高阶动力学可被有效成对模型准确再现；不同相互作用机制产生几乎无法区分的动力学，存在机制可识别性问题。

Conclusion: 仅凭时间序列数据不足以可靠推断相互作用结构，需补充额外的生态信息。

Abstract: Higher-order interactions are increasingly recognized as a key component of ecological dynamics. However, we show that higher-order Lotka-Volterra dynamics can, in some scenarios, be accurately reproduced by effective pairwise models fitted to the same abundance time series. Consequently, higher-order interactions cannot, in general, be inferred from time-series data alone. We further identify a fundamental problem of mechanistic identifiability, whereby distinct interaction mechanisms generate nearly indistinguishable dynamics, potentially leading to accurate yet misleading ecological interpretations. Our results highlight the need to complement time-series data with additional ecological information to infer interaction structure reliably.

</details>
